# Paseo 0.15.4 feature catalogue

Survey consolidation; source inspection only. Vessel node: `d954fa3a`; native app and page: `1d1f11df`. No builds or runtime checks.

Coverage: every `##` and `###` in the 17 requested survey files, including section-index headings, plus each inventory item whose normalized wording does not already have a heading in that file. Semicolon-separated flat inventories in settings/plugins are expanded. Repeated coverage across survey files is retained as separate source occurrences; the total is an occurrence count, not a deduplicated product-feature count. `area-7-cli-protocol.md` supplies message names only and contributes no feature rows.

`present` means the named source surface exists for this small feature; a plain native path is present. `partial` identifies a narrower/different source implementation; it is not full Paseo parity. `none` means no matching surface was found in the inspected source, including explicitly disabled routes. `unclear` means static inspection did not establish the match within the bounded lookup. Node names are related serving methods from the pinned method table, not a promise of identical contracts. `none` in the message column means no explicit wire-message name was established in that feature text (local/CLI/internal features may have none). Source refs use GitHub-style heading anchors.

## Evidence boundaries

- All method names were checked against `d954fa3a:crates/vessel-proto/src/rpc.rs`. Native/page paths were checked against the committed tree and their source read/searched at `1d1f11df`; tests, fixtures and comments alone do not establish a working UI.
- Native route availability: `spikes/native/app/src/areas/settings/sections.rs:45` registers renderers and explicitly returns `None` for Terminal, Browser, Editor, Files, Integrations, Notifications, Permissions, Metadata, Voice, Workspaces, Usage, Terminals, Plugins and Models settings. Their model/type names do not count as pages.
- Native schedules: `spikes/native/app/src/areas/sidebar/mod.rs:279` returns `None` from the schedule slot; scheduling methods exist on the node.
- Native host-agent settings: `spikes/native/app/src/areas/settings/host_agents.rs:1` implements idle release only and explicitly omits Paseo tools, browser tools, append prompt, restart recovery, skills and profiles.
- Native attachments: `spikes/native/app/src/areas/composer/files.rs:150` restricts the attach menu to image/file; forge issue and plugin rows are absent.
- Native slash menu: `spikes/native/app/src/areas/composer/slash.rs:1` uses cached commands for drafts because harness options do not expose them.
- Native PR UI: `spikes/native/app/src/areas/pulls/mod.rs:1` uses manual-title dialogs for facts and creation, rather than a full PR tab/timeline. `pr.feedback` sends failing forge checks/reviews; it does not prove Paseo inline review comments.
- Native appearance: `spikes/native/app/src/areas/settings/appearance.rs:1` documents System/Light/Dark only, with limited size/width controls and no font-family/syntax picker.
- `unclear` and source-index rows remain in totals and are reported separately; they are not counted as `none`.
